Abstract: We present a demonstration showcasing the Autonomy Testbed for Multi-purpose Orbiting Systems (ATMOS), a planar spacecraft-analog robot designed for hardware-in-the-loop evaluation of guidance and control strategies in microgravity-like conditions. Using ATMOS as the physical test platform, we investigate the design, analysis, and performance evaluation of control architectures for remotely operated spacecraft under round-trip communication delays. In this work, we develop and experimentally validate a control strategy that combines state prediction and trajectory tracking control to perform a docking maneuver, accounting for time-varying random communication latency between ground operators and the ATMOS system. The demonstration includes a long-distance remote control experiment between Seoul and Stockholm, introducing realistic intercontinental delays and variability. The results highlight the capability of ATMOS to support rapid, reliable, and cost-effective testing of spacecraft teleoperation concepts, establishing a first step toward robust validation of on-orbit operations in microgravity-like environments.

</details>


### [109] [Evolve Vision-Language-Action Model into an Agent with On-the-fly Tool-use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14047)
*Yi Ding,Yanzhao Yu,Xili Dai,Xianbiao Qi,Peiwen Sun,Xueqian Wang,Xiangyu Yue,Jianan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了Agentic Robot with Tool-use (ART)框架，通过将视觉-语言-动作模型与代理工具使用相结合，提升机器人在复杂环境中的泛化能力和数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在处理复杂任务时面临动作空间过大、数据依赖性强和泛化能力不足的问题。因此，需要一种能够降低动作空间复杂度、减少数据需求并提高跨任务泛化能力的新方法。

Method: 提出ART框架，通过引入现成的工具模块（用于低级视觉、高级功能和具身增强），对VLA模型进行微调以支持工具使用；构建包含3万个工具使用轨迹的数据集，并设计适用于长轨迹推理的训练方案，在模拟和真实环境中进行实验验证。

Result: ART在模拟和真实世界任务中比主流基线方法成功率高出20%，例如在黑暗环境中从新视角执行抓取放置任务；同时展现出更高的数据效率、更轻量的部署和可扩展的工具集成能力。

Conclusion: ART框架通过代理式工具使用显著提升了VLA模型的通用性、鲁棒性和可扩展性，为VLA系统在复杂现实场景中的实际部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: This paper integrates end-to-end Visual-Language-Action (VLA) models with agentic tool-use to propose Agentic Robot with Tool-use (ART). ART is a tool-injection framework that tunes any VLA model to leverage off-the-shelf tool modules for low-level vision, high-level affordance, and embodiment enhancement. Compared to vanilla VLA models with a whole continuous action solution space, ART reduces the complexity of the action solution space through tool-use, which not only improves generalizability across different tasks but also reduces data dependency. To demonstrate the advantages (high generalizability and low data dependency) of this framework, we first built a dataset of 30K tool-use trajectories and action demonstrations, which is much smaller than those used by baseline methods. We then designed a training regimen for long-trajectory tool-use reasoning in challenging environments. Experiments show that ART achieves a 20% higher success rate than mainstream baselines on simulation and real-world tasks, such as pick-and-place in the dark at novel viewpoints. Empirical results highlight the benefits of an agent-based approach: modular tool utilization enables more efficient training, lightweight deployment, and scalable integration of new tools. This design fosters robustness, adaptability, and extensibility, paving the way for the practical deployment of VLA systems in complex real-world scenarios.

</details>


### [110] [FlatLab: A Unified Methodology Framework and Simulation-Based Benchmark for Robotic Manipulation of Flat Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14049)
*Xingyu Zhu,Wenshuo Han,Zhouyu Wang,Yuran Wang,Ruihai Wu,Hao Dong,Fan Tang,Hechang Chen,Hyung Jin Chang,Yixing Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种解耦策略生成和动作执行的统一框架，用于机器人对平面物体的操作，并在新构建的仿真基准FlatLab上验证了其在未见物体和类别上的良好泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平面物体因几何和材质变化大、难以抓取而使机器人操作困难，现有方法依赖启发式预操作且泛化能力有限。

Method: 将操作解耦为策略生成器和动作执行模块：策略生成器通过模拟数据变换和对比学习，从点云中学习策略中心、对象不变的表示以预测操作策略；执行模块则基于预测策略将长视野操作分解为可重用的动作原语并动态组合生成稳定轨迹。

Result: 在新提出的FlatLab仿真基准上进行实验，该基准支持多种刚性和可变形平面物体的高保真物理仿真、自动化多模态数据采集及标准化任务与评估协议，结果表明所提方法在未见物体和类别上优于现有基线。

Conclusion: 所提出的解耦框架结合策略级表征学习与动作原语动态组合，在平面物体操作任务中实现了更强的泛化能力和有效性，FlatLab为该领域提供了标准化的评估平台。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation of flat objects is challenging due to the ungraspable configurations and strong variations in object geometry and material. Existing methods rely on heuristic pre-manipulation and are often evaluated in closed settings with limited generalization. We propose a unified framework that decouples the manipulation into a strategy generator and an action execution module. The strategy generator predicts appropriate manipulation strategies from object point clouds by learning strategy-centric, object-invariant representations via simulated data transformation and contrastive learning. Conditioned on the predicted strategy, the execution module decomposes long-horizon manipulation into reusable action primitives and dynamically composes them to generate stable trajectories. To enable systematic evaluation, we introduce FlatLab, a comprehensive simulation benchmark for robotic flat object manipulation. FlatLab provides high-fidelity physical simulation of diverse rigid and deformable flat objects, automated multi-modal data collection, and standardized task definitions and evaluation protocols. Experiments conducted in FlatLab demonstrate that our approach generalizes effectively to unseen objects and categories, outperforming existing baselines. The project page and the code are provided at https://flatlab-web.github.io/.

</details>


### [111] [PILOT: Privileged Imitation Learning for End-to-End Motion Planning of Autonomous UAVs under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14082)
*Qingrui Zhang,Feng Xue,Xiang Zhou,Chenghao Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为PILOT的约束感知模仿学习框架，用于在部分可观测环境中实现基于视觉的无人机端到端运动规划，结合时空感知融合与轨迹参数化，在降低计算开销的同时实现了接近专家水平的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在杂乱动态环境中，由于感知受限和系统动力学约束，自主导航面临挑战，现有方法难以兼顾实时性、安全性与泛化能力。

Method: 提出PILOT框架，利用特权模仿学习将最优控制专家策略的知识迁移到学生策略中；采用时间卷积网络（TCN）融合历史深度图像与里程计信息以缓解部分可观测问题；设计双目标损失函数正则化策略，并通过轨迹参数化层确保输出轨迹的动力学一致性与约束满足。

Result: 在四旋翼和固定翼飞行器的仿真中，PILOT性能接近特权专家且计算开销降低80%以上；零样本室内外部署实验验证了其实际可行性与跨域泛化能力。

Conclusion: PILOT有效解决了部分可观测与动力学约束下的无人机导航问题，无需建图即可提升空间感知与规划安全性，具备良好的实时性与部署潜力。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation in cluttered environments is hampered by partial observability and dynamic constraints. This paper presents PILOT, a constraint-aware privileged imitation learning framework for vision-based end-to-end UAV motion planning under partial observability. The framework distills planning strategies from a computationally intensive optimal control expert into a student policy regularized toward safety and dynamic requirements via a dual-objective loss function. To mitigate partial observability, a spatiotemporal perception fusion module using a Temporal Convolutional Network (TCN) is developed to integrate historical depth images and odometry. This module infers task-relevant latent context from historical observations, enhancing spatial awareness beyond the instantaneous FOV without maintaining persistent map memory. A trajectory parameterization layer mapping network outputs to a structured trajectory, while enabling explicit continuity, dynamic-consistency, and obstacle soft penalties during training, encouraging constraint satisfaction for unseen observations without formal guarantees. Simulations on quadrotor and fixed-wing aircraft demonstrate that PILOT achieves performance comparable to the privileged expert while reducing computational overhead by over 80\%. Successful indoor and outdoor zero-shot deployment confirms the practical feasibility and cross-domain generalization of the planner.

</details>


### [112] [AgilePE: Autonomous UAV Pursuit-Evasion via Self-Play Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14135)
*Wenhao Tang,Tianyang Chen,Zhejun Cui,Boyuan An,Jiayu Chen,Ruize Zhang,Huidong Liu,Tianyue Wu,Qingmin Liao,Fei Gao,Yu Wang,Chao Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于自博弈强化学习的无人机自主追逃系统AgilePE，实现了从仿真到现实的零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的规则方法和微分博弈在高维空战交互和敏捷机动中表现不佳，难以应对动态对手行为。

Method: 采用竞争性自博弈训练框架，结合优先级虚拟自博弈（PFSP）和多样化对手池，直接从机载状态观测输出集体推力和机体角速度（CTBR）指令，实现端到端控制。

Result: 在仿真中涌现出复杂的追逃策略，所学策略无需微调即可零样本迁移到真实四旋翼平台，实验验证了快速闪避、包抄等战术的有效性。

Conclusion: AgilePE为无人机自主追逃提供了一个完整的端到端解决方案，展示了强化学习在复杂空中对抗任务中的潜力。

Abstract: Autonomous pursuit-evasion is a fundamental challenge for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs), requiring rapid decision-making under tightly coupled dynamics and continuously changing opponent behaviors. Traditional rule-based or differential-game approaches often struggle with high-dimensional aerial interactions and agile maneuvering. We present AgilePE, a complete system for autonomous UAV pursuit-evasion via self-play reinforcement learning. AgilePE integrates agile low-level control, competitive policy optimization, and sim-to-real deployment in a unified framework. The policy directly maps onboard state observations to Collective Thrust and Body Rates (CTBR) commands, enabling end-to-end agile maneuvering without intermediate trajectory planners or waypoint controllers. For training, we use competitive self-play with Prioritized Fictitious Self-Play (PFSP) and a diversified opponent pool, enabling agents to improve against historical policies while stabilizing optimization and reducing policy oscillation. This process leads to the emergence of sophisticated pursuit and evasion strategies. For real-world deployment, we develop a hardware-aligned simulation pipeline that models actuator-response dynamics, communication latency, and domain randomization. The learned policies transfer zero-shot to real quadrotors without task-specific tuning. Real-world experiments reproduce pursuit-evasion tactics observed in simulation, including rapid dodging and flanking, and demonstrate interactive two-agent zero-shot deployment.

</details>


### [113] [OccPlanner: Goal-Aware Occupancy-Conditioned Diffusion Planner for Pixel-Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14160)
*Binling Huang,Nianjin Ye,Xi Yang,Liang Hu,Zhou Huang,Shuang Wei,Longrui Yang,Yanchi Chen,Lanpeng Jia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为OccPlanner的新型导航方法，能够将像素级目标映射到三维空间中，并结合局部3D占据信息进行连续规划，在仿真和真实环境中均表现出优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素级导航目标缺乏深度和可通行性信息，导致3D目标定位和无碰撞规划困难，因此需要一种能有效理解环境占据并实现精确导航的方法。

Method: 提出OccPlanner，利用目标感知的占据条件扩散模型，结合时间视觉上下文和学习到的局部3D占据特征进行目标定位与路径规划；并通过L3ROcc方法从单目视频生成大规模机器人中心的3D占据标注用于训练。

Result: 在四个未见场景类别中，5-8米距离范围内，OccPlanner将平均成功率从20.81%提升至71.55%，在杂乱环境中分别达到86.20%和84.92%的成功率；实机实验验证了其在真实世界的可行性与迁移能力。

Conclusion: OccPlanner通过融合视觉上下文与3D占据感知，显著提升了像素目标导航的性能，展示了良好的仿真到现实迁移潜力。

Abstract: Pixel-goal navigation specifies targets directly in the agent's camera view, but a target pixel provides neither metric depth nor traversability, making 3D goal grounding and collision-free continuous planning challenging. We present OccPlanner, a goal-aware occupancy-conditioned diffusion planner that grounds pixel goals in egocentric metric space and sequentially conditions the goal representation on temporal visual context and learned local 3D occupancy features. To provide occupancy supervision at scale, we introduce L3ROcc, which converts monocular RGB navigation videos into robot-centric local 3D occupancy annotations through geometric reconstruction and ray-based visibility reasoning. We train OccPlanner on InternData-N1 and evaluate it in closed-loop simulation across four unseen scene categories from InternScenes and two goal-distance ranges. In the 5-8 m setting, OccPlanner increases the average success rate (SR) over NavDP from 20.81% to 71.55% across the four categories, reaching 86.20% and 84.92% in cluttered-easy and cluttered-hard scenes, respectively. Real-world open-loop experiments on a Unitree Go2 further provide initial evidence of sim-to-real transfer and adaptation with L3ROcc-generated supervision.

</details>


### [114] [MMUSV-Sim: A Perception-Oriented Simulation and Data-Generation Platform for Multi-USV Cooperative Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14207)
*Ziao Li,Jianxiong Ye,Biao Tang,Leping Zhang,Kun Zuo,Siyu Huang,Chenqiang Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向多无人水面艇（USV）协同感知的仿真与数据生成平台MMUSV-Sim，基于Unreal Engine 5和AirSim构建，支持多种海洋环境、多模态传感器数据采集和统一标注，实验表明该平台能有效提升协同感知性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在大规模场景下开发多USV协同感知系统，需要一个统一且可配置的仿真平台，以支持多平台协作、多模态感知和共享标注，弥补真实海上数据稀缺和测试成本高的问题。

Method: 基于Unreal Engine 5和AirSim构建MMUSV-Sim平台，提供多种海洋环境、天气与波浪条件、船舶资产库及基于样条的多船运动控制；同步采集RGB、深度、语义、LiDAR和雷达数据，并记录全局世界状态用于生成各智能体的标注数据。

Result: 实验证明所配置的波浪参数能准确影响船只的纵摇、横摇和垂荡运动，且投影标注与语义渲染之间具有良好的几何一致性；在生成的多USV数据集上进行LiDAR协同BEV检测实验，Early Fusion方法的AP@0.5达到72.74，显著高于单USV的45.54。

Conclusion: MMUSV-Sim是一个功能全面、感知导向的多USV仿真平台，能够有效支持复杂海上场景下的协同感知算法开发与评估，显著提升检测性能，具备应用于未来海上自主系统研究的潜力。

Abstract: Cooperative perception among multiple unmanned surface vehicles (USVs) combines complementary observations to extend maritime target sensing beyond the view range and field of a single platform. Developing such systems at scale calls for a unified workflow for configurable multi-USV scenarios, multimodal acquisition, and shared annotations. We present MMUSV-Sim, a perception-oriented maritime simulation and data-generation platform built on Unreal Engine 5 and Project AirSim. It provides island, open-sea, and port environments; configurable weather, time of day, and wave conditions; a diverse vessel asset library; and spline-based multi-vessel motion. MMUSV-Sim acquires RGB, depth, semantic, LiDAR, and radar observations across multiple USVs and captures a common world state for per-agent annotation export. Experiments verify that the configured wave settings produce the intended changes in vessel heave, roll, and pitch, and evaluate the geometric consistency between projected annotations and semantic renderings. In LiDAR-based cooperative BEV vessel detection experiments on the generated multi-USV dataset, Early Fusion achieves an AP@0.5 of 72.74, compared with 45.54 using a single USV.

</details>


### [115] [Vibration Suppression in Collaborative Flexible Payload Manipulation Using Passive Force Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14244)
*Alaa Abderrahim,Antonio Rosales,Ferdinando Milella,Markku Suomalainen,Shuai Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于主从机器人协作的柔性负载振动抑制控制策略，通过速度整形和导纳控制实现对大型结构振动的有效抑制，并验证了方法的稳定性和有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大型重型结构运动过程中会产生振动，影响精确操作，尤其是在未来聚变反应堆等前沿项目中，亟需有效的振动抑制方法。

Method: 采用两个工业机械臂构成主从配置，主机器人通过 shaped velocity commands 引导运动，从机器人利用导纳控制器补偿主机器人对负载施加的外力估计，从而抑制横向振动；系统动力学建模为等效质量-弹簧-阻尼模型，并分析能量耗散与稳定性。

Result: 数值仿真验证了该方法的有效性，实验结果表明该策略能有效抑制柔性负载的振动，适用于更重、更大且具有异构机器人参数的系统。

Conclusion: 所提协作操控策略能够有效抑制大柔度负载在运动过程中的振动，具备良好的稳定性和适用性，为复杂环境下重型结构的操作提供了可行解决方案。

Abstract: In large and heavy structures, vibrations arise during motion, posing significant challenges for precise manipulation. To accomplish the desired motion, control algorithms must effectively suppress these structural vibrations. In cutting edge projects, such as remote maintenance of future fusion energy reactors (tokamaks), the manipulation of this type of structure is defined as a crucial task. This paper presents a control strategy to suppress transverse vibrations in flexible payloads during motion using a collaborative payload manipulation approach. Two different industrial robot arms are arranged in a leader follower configuration for the manipulation strategy. The leader robot guides the motion with shaped velocity commands, while the follower robot ensures compliance with the estimated external forces applied by the leader on the payload through an admittance controller. Unlike existing methods, the proposed approach enables collaborative manipulation of heavier and larger flexible objects, addressing additional challenges such as vibration suppression and heterogeneous robot specifications. The dynamics of the leader follower payload system are modeled using an equivalent mass spring damper model, and it is shown that, with appropriate admittance parameters, the total energy of the system is passively dissipated. A stability proof is also provided. Numerical simulations validate the proposed method, and experimental results demonstrate its effectiveness.

</details>


### [116] [Accelerating Large-scale Bundle Adjustment for LiDAR Mapping via Parallel Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14266)
*Yixi Cai,Rundong Li,Yuhan Xie,Qingwen Zhang,Patric Jensfelt,Fu Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个用于大规模地图构建的全并行LiDAR捆绑调整计算框架，通过自适应异步数据加载、自底向上体素化提取平面特征以及基于优化算法的并行计算，显著提升了计算效率，同时保持了高精度的地图构建效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提升大规模LiDAR点云地图构建中捆绑调整的计算效率，克服传统方法在处理大规模数据时的计算瓶颈和内存限制。

Method: 提出了一种全并行计算框架，包括：1）自适应异步数据加载策略以应对GPU内存限制；2）新的自底向上体素化方法用于并行提取平面特征；3）基于majorization-minimization优化框架，实现残差、雅可比矩阵、Hessian矩阵计算及增量求解的并行化。

Result: 在多个大规模公开数据集和不同计算平台上进行了广泛测试，计算效率最高提升了十倍，同时保持与现有最先进方法相当的建图精度。

Conclusion: 所提出的全并行框架显著加速了大规模LiDAR捆绑调整，具有良好的鲁棒性、适应性和实际应用价值，代码已开源以促进后续研究。

Abstract: LiDAR bundle adjustment is widely utilized in mapping to construct globally consistent point cloud maps. In this paper, we propose the first fully parallel computing framework to accelerate LiDAR bundle adjustment for large-scale mapping, incorporating three key techniques. First, we design an adaptive, asynchronous data loading strategy to efficiently process large-scale point cloud datasets on memory-constrained GPUs. Secondly, we present a novel bottom-up voxelization method for extracting planar features, enabling fully parallelized pre-processing. Thirdly, we build upon a majorization-minimization formulation to accelerate compute-intensive tasks in the optimization via parallel computation, including the computation of residuals, Jacobian and Hessian matrices, and a parallel increment solver. To support our design, we provide both theoretical and experimental analysis of the time complexity of our approach. Extensive benchmarking on large-scale public datasets across various computational platforms validates the robustness and adaptability of our approach, achieving up to a tenfold improvement in computational efficiency while preserving mapping accuracy comparable to state-of-the-art methods. To benefit future research, the implementation code is available on GitHub.

</details>


### [117] [PRM-as-a-Judge 1.5: A Toolkit for Robot Process Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14284)
*Yuyang Liu,Yanqing Shen,Ruike Chen,Jifan Zhao,Yuxuan Tian,Yichi Zhang,Tianfeng Long,Zixuan Yin,Yipu Wang,Ziheng Qin,Wenxing Tan,Yang Shi,Mingyu Cao,Runze Xiao,Ziqi Wang,Zhixin Yin,Shiwei Chu,Yi-Fan Zhang,Yao Mu,Yuheng Ji,Yihao Wang,Jun Yan,Zhongyuan Wang,Pengwei Wang,Xiaolong Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PRM-as-a-Judge 1.5是一个用于机器人过程评估的工具包，通过回放视频生成细粒度进度曲线和多种精细指标，提升对具身模型能力的理解，并推出RoboPulse++以提高过程奖励模型评估的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人评估多依赖二元成功率和基于规则的过程评分，缺乏对执行过程中细粒度进展的刻画，难以全面反映具身模型的真实能力。

Method: 提出PRM-as-a-Judge 1.5工具包，利用基准测试中的 rollout 视频生成密集进度曲线，引入三个新指标：失败侧进展、回落后恢复和成功侧执行质量；并构建RoboPulse++评估过程奖励模型的可靠性。

Result: 实现了对具身模型的全面细粒度评估，提供了可量化的精细指标结果和关键发现，并发布了包含基准、指标实现和可视化工具的用户友好评估套件。

Conclusion: 推动社区重新思考机器人评估方式，倡导建立透明、可重复、注重过程的评估体系，为下一代具身智能的发展奠定基础。

Abstract: Fine-grained robotic evaluation matters for understanding embodied models, going beyond binary success rates and rule-based process scores. We present PRM-as-a-Judge 1.5, a toolkit for robot process assessment that turns rollout videos into dense progress curves and derives multiple fine metrics. PRM-as-a-Judge 1.5 introduces three metrics, building on version 1.0, that characterize failure-side progress, post-drawdown recovery, and success-side execution quality, helping users understand embodied model capability. Based on the rollout videos from benchmarks, we perform a comprehensive assessment of the embodied models, providing some fine-grained metric results and key findings. We further introduce RoboPulse++ to evaluate the reliability of process reward models (PRM), providing evaluators with a more accurate testing platform. Moreover, we release a user-friendly assessment suite, including the benchmark, metric implementation, and visualization tools, to support reproducible manipulation process evaluation. We call on the community to rethink how robots are evaluated and establish transparent, procedural, and reproducible assessment as a foundation for the next generation of embodied intelligence.

</details>


### [118] [CORAL: Curriculum-Optimized Reward Adaptation for LiDAR-Based Goal-Directed Urban Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14332)
*Anisa Saleem,Duksu Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为CORAL的强化学习方法，用于解决自动驾驶中长距离目标导航的复杂任务。通过设计五阶段课程学习和阶段感知奖励机制，CORAL在CARLA仿真环境中显著提升了导航成功率，并实现了跨城市的零样本迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶中的长距离目标导航需要策略同时掌握多种竞争性行为（如避障、遵守信号、路径跟踪等），而传统固定目标无法提供有效的学习顺序，导致训练困难。

Method: 提出CORAL框架，包含两个协同推进的调度机制：一是五阶段课程学习，逐步增加路线长度并收紧行为约束；二是阶段感知奖励函数，动态调整不同奖励成分的权重，以适应任务难度的变化。采用基于PPO算法的多流Actor-Critic网络，在99维紧凑状态空间上进行训练，状态包括极坐标LiDAR直方图、车辆遥测、自车坐标系下的路径几何和交通规则指示器，无需点云编码或鸟瞰图栅格化。

Result: 在相同协议下对比两种PPO基线方法，CORAL在最长路线和完整行为约束下所有20个评估场景均成功到达目标，而基线仅达到5%和10%的成功率；消融实验表明，单独移除任一调度机制都会降低成功率和路径完成度，同时禁用两者将使成功率降至55%；在一个城市训练的策略可零样本迁移到七个未见城市，在100-150米路线上的成功率为68%-98%，平均横向偏差低于0.35米。

Conclusion: CORAL通过课程学习与动态奖励调度的有效结合，显著提升了复杂城市环境下自动驾驶策略的学习效率与泛化能力，验证了结构化训练策略在长视野导航任务中的关键作用。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is promising for autonomous urban driving, but long-horizon goal-directed navigation asks a policy to acquire several competing behaviors at once--reaching a distant goal, tracking a route, avoiding obstacles, obeying signals--and a fixed objective gives no order in which to learn them. This paper presents CORAL, which advances two schedules together: a five-stage curriculum that progressively lengthens routes and tightens behavioral constraints, and a stage-aware reward whose component weights shift emphasis from mission progress toward route following, safety, smoothness, and rule compliance as the task hardens. The policy is a multi-stream actor-critic network trained with Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) in CARLA on a compact 99-dimensional state pairing a polar LiDAR histogram with vehicle telemetry, ego-frame route geometry, and traffic-rule indicators--no point-cloud encoder, no bird's-eye-view rasterization. Against two PPO baselines under an identical protocol, CORAL reaches the goal in all twenty evaluation episodes on the longest routes under the full set of behavioral constraints, where the baselines reach 5% and 10%; a factorial ablation shows that neither schedule alone matches their combination: removing either lowers both success and route completion, and disabling both drops success to 55%. Trained in one town, the policy transfers zero-shot to seven unseen towns, succeeding in 68-98% of episodes on routes of the same 100-150 m length, with mean lateral deviation below 0.35 m.

</details>


### [119] [Reflex: Enabling Fast and Predictive Vision-Language-Action Models for Reaction-Critical Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14379)
*Yuxuan Chen,Wanruo Zhang,Xiao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了ReflexBench，一个用于反应关键型操作的基准测试，并设计了ReflexVLA模型以提升在动态交互场景中的性能，兼顾低延迟部署与高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的基准主要关注静态操作任务，忽视了对动态交互场景的泛化能力评估。为此，作者希望填补这一空白，推动机器人在反应关键型任务中的发展。

Method: 提出ReflexBench，包含六个动态任务，并设计解耦仿真步进与机器人控制的评估框架，支持同步和异步推理下的可配置延迟；在此基础上开发ReflexVLA模型，通过潜在未来预测和多帧时序融合增强时序推理能力，并利用批量视觉编码和CUDA Graph回放降低部署延迟。

Result: 实验表明，ReflexVLA在动态操作任务中性能显著提升，同时在标准静态基准上保持竞争力；真实世界实验验证了其在实际部署条件下的有效性。

Conclusion: ReflexVLA结合高效的架构设计，在无需大规模机器人数据预训练的情况下，实现了在动态和静态操作任务中的良好表现，展示了其在反应关键型机器人应用中的潜力。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have recently achieved promising performance in robotic manipulation. However, existing benchmarks mainly evaluate generalization on static manipulation tasks and largely overlook dynamic interaction scenarios. To address this gap, we present ReflexBench, a benchmark for reaction-critical manipulation. ReflexBench contains six dynamic tasks and introduces an evaluation framework that decouples simulator stepping from robot control while supporting configurable latency under synchronous and asynchronous inference. Building upon ReflexBench, we propose ReflexVLA, an efficient VLA model designed for reaction-critical manipulation without large-scale robot-data pretraining. ReflexVLA enhances temporal reasoning through latent future prediction and multi-frame temporal fusion within the vision backbone, while reducing deployment latency through batched visual encoding and CUDA Graph replay. Experiments show that ReflexVLA consistently improves dynamic manipulation performance while maintaining competitive accuracy on standard static manipulation benchmarks, and real-world experiments further demonstrate its effectiveness under practical deployment conditions. Project website: https://reflexvla.github.io

</details>


### [120] [Effect of Twisted-Yarn Architecture on Pressure and Proximity Sensing Characteristics of Textile Capacitive Sensors for Robotic Skin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14406)
*Ishtia Zahir,Eslam Saleh,Maryam Rezayati,Güunter Grabher,Gaffar Hossain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了纱线层级结构对纺织电容传感器性能的影响，提出了一种基于银涂层纱线和聚二甲基硅氧烷的多层编织电容传感平台。随着层数增加，传感器的机械强度、灵敏度和稳定性显著提升，并揭示了结构依赖的灵敏度-探测范围权衡关系，实现了压力、接近感知及机器人实时交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有纺织集成电容传感器中，纱线层级结构对电容转导特性的影响缺乏量化分析，限制了其在可穿戴电子和人机交互中的优化应用。

Method: 采用银涂层纱线涂覆聚二甲基硅氧烷，构建单层、双层和四层 twisted 结构的纺织电容传感器，系统研究有效电极重叠面积和纤维间距对电容响应的影响，并集成为4×4传感阵列用于空间触觉映射和机器人实时检测。

Result: 层数增加显著提升性能：断裂伸长率从37.5%增至85.0%，最大载荷从23.3 N增至89.7 N；四层传感器在100 kHz下灵敏度达0.1331 MPa⁻¹，滞后低、热漂移小，循环稳定性超过15,000次；接近检测范围分别为60 mm（单层）、50 mm（双层）、40 mm（四层），存在灵敏度与范围的权衡；机器人集成系统端到端延迟为403 ms。

Conclusion: 纱线架构是调控纺织集成电容传感系统测量特性的关键可调参数，可通过层数设计实现机械性能、灵敏度、稳定性和功能性的协同优化。

Abstract: Textile-integrated capacitive sensors offer flexible and conformable tactile sensing for wearable electronics and human-robot interaction; however, the influence of yarn-level architecture on capacitive transduction characteristics remains insufficiently quantified. This work presents a textile capacitive sensing platform based on silver-coated yarns coated with polydimethylsiloxane and assembled into one-, two-, and four-layer twisted configurations. The influence of effective electrode overlap area and inter-fiber separation on the capacitive response is systematically investigated, enabling architecture-dependent tuning of pressure and proximity sensing characteristics. Pressure was calculated using the localized single-fiber contact area, corresponding to stresses of 0.4-3.9 MPa. Increasing the layer number improved mechanical strength and sensing performance: elongation at break increased from 37.5% to 62.5% and 85.0%, while the maximum load increased from 23.3 to 42.7 and 89.7 N. Sensitivity increased with layer number and frequency, reaching 0.1331 MPa$^{-1}$ for the four-layer sensor at 100 kHz. The four-layer configuration also exhibited low hysteresis, minimal thermal drift from 25 to 90 $^\circ$C, and stable operation over 15,000 cycles. Proximity detection ranges of 60, 50, and 40 mm were obtained for the one-, two-, and four-layer sensors, respectively, revealing an architecture-dependent sensitivity-range trade-off. A 4$\times$4 textile sensing array enabled spatial contact mapping, while robotic-arm integration demonstrated real-time touch and proximity detection with an end-to-end robotic system latency (from detection to robot reaction) of 403 ms. The results establish yarn architecture as a tunable design parameter governing the measurement characteristics of textile-integrated capacitive sensing systems.

</details>


### [121] [Control-Informed Constraint Adaptation in Minimum-Time Trajectory Planning for Autonomous Racing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14448)
*Ann-Kathrin Schwehn,Alexander Langmann,Mattia Piccinini,Johannes Betz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种控制感知的在线轨迹规划框架，通过学习自身执行误差动态调整空间约束，提升自动驾驶赛车性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统轨迹规划器假设完美跟踪，忽视执行误差，导致保守的空间裕度，无法充分利用赛道空间。

Method: 在运行时测量系统性跟踪偏差，动态调整赛道的空间约束，迭代扩展自由空间规划区域，同时保持时间最优性并补偿累积的执行误差。

Result: 在高保真闭环仿真环境中验证，该方法将圈速缩短1.8秒，中位运行时间保持25毫秒，未增加计算负担。

Conclusion: 将控制偏差反馈至规划层可突破模块化架构的性能瓶颈，使自动驾驶车辆系统性地利用赛道极限。

Abstract: Autonomous racecars operate at the limits of vehicle dynamics, where small control errors translate into safety-critical behavior and lost performance. Trajectory planners assume perfect tracking and remain blind to execution errors. To guarantee safety, trajectory planners therefore restrict themselves to conservative spatial margins, leaving usable track space untapped. To overcome these issues, we introduce a control-informed online trajectory planning framework that learns from its own execution errors. By measuring systematic tracking deviations during runtime, we dynamically adapt spatial track constraints and iteratively expand the free-space planning area. The planner remains time-optimal while compensating for accumulated execution errors. This method was analyzed in a high-fidelity closed-loop simulation environment with autonomous racecars. The results demonstrate that our approach reduces lap time by 1.8\,s without increasing computational burden, maintaining a median runtime of 25 ms. Our finding indicates that feeding control-induced deviations back into the planning layer unlocks performance previously inaccessible to modular architectures and enables autonomous vehicles to exploit track limits systematically.

</details>


### [122] [THRIVE: Therapeutic Humanoid Robot In Virtual Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14462)
*Jin Xu,Yu-Ping Chen,Ayanna Howard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: THRIVE是一个结合虚拟现实游戏、实时动作捕捉和社交机器人治疗师的居家康复平台，旨在为上肢运动障碍儿童提供持续且有趣的康复训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了改善患有上肢运动功能障碍儿童的康复效果，需要一种能够在家庭环境中实现一致、任务特异性训练的解决方案。

Method: THRIVE集成了定制的虚拟现实康复游戏、基于摄像头的动作追踪系统，以及可作为实体机器人或远程虚拟代理的机器人治疗师，通过模块化、机器人无关的设计支持多种机器人形态。

Result: 系统能够实时捕捉儿童运动表现，并由机器人 therapist 提供自适应反馈，提升参与度和运动准确性；模块化设计增强了系统的可扩展性与家庭适用性。

Conclusion: THRIVE为儿童上肢康复提供了一个经济、可扩展且易于在家庭中长期使用的智能化解决方案，具有广泛的临床应用前景。

Abstract: This paper presents THRIVE (Therapeutic Humanoid Robot In Virtual Environment), an at-home rehabilitation platform that integrates a suite of virtual-reality upper-body rehabilitation games, a real-time camera-based motion-tracking system, and a socially interactive robot therapist. The system is designed for therapy and intervention in children with upper-limb motor impairments, which can be improved through consistent, task-specific practice. THRIVE features a set of newly designed, engaging games that target functional reaching, grasping, and object-manipulation movements through customizable popping, hitting, catching, and grabbing tasks, while the camera-based tracking system captures the child's kinematic performance during play. A robot therapist - deployable either as a physical robotic coach or as a remote-presence virtual agent - delivers adaptive, dynamic feedback to motivate the child and guide their movements toward therapeutic goals. THRIVE decouples the therapeutic games from the robot embodiment, extending the platform to support various embodiments and different robots within one modular system. This robot-agnostic design makes THRIVE affordable, scalable, and readily adaptable for sustained use in the home, offering a practical pathway to more consistent and engaging upper-limb therapy for children with motor function impairments.

</details>


### [123] [Expected Free Energy-based Informative Path Planning for Robotic Mars Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14466)
*Ajith Anil Meera,Pablo Lanillos,Wouter Kouw*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于预期自由能（EFE）的机器人路径规划方法，用于在资源受限条件下同时实现环境信息采集与高价值区域探索，优于传统信息论方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人在未知环境中需同时兼顾构建精确信息图与快速发现高价值区域，而传统方法仅单独优化信息获取或奖励获取，缺乏统一的有效准则。

Method: 采用主动推理中的预期自由能（EFE）作为统一决策目标，在高斯过程信念下进行连续轨迹规划，并施加严格的路径长度约束以控制预算。

Result: 实验结果表明，基于EFE的规划方法在相同条件下能同时生成准确的后验地图并定位最高价值区域，性能优于传统信息论基线方法。

Conclusion: EFE为资源受限的机器人探索提供了一种统一、易于调节且符合原则的信息收集策略，有助于实现高效自主部署。

Abstract: An autonomous robot efficiently exploring an unknown environment, such as looking for water sources on Mars, faces two simultaneous demands: building an accurate information map while quickly finding the regions of greatest value, and paying for every meter of travel and the cost of every measurement it takes. Classical information-seeking and reward-seeking criteria address only one of these objectives at a time. Here, we propose Expected Free Energy (EFE), the principled action-selection objective from active inference, as a unifying criterion for budgeted robotic informative path planning. Maintaining a Gaussian-process belief over the information field, our agent plans continuous trajectories that minimize expected free energy under hard path-length constraints. The results from multiple realizations show that EFE-based planning yields accurate posterior maps and locates the highest-value regions simultaneously, outperforming information-theoretic baselines under the same settings. In robotic exploration, these unified, easy-to-tune principled information-gathering strategies facilitate autonomous deployment while enforcing efficiency and resource constraints.

</details>


### [124] [Ensuring Safe Physical AI in Urban Mobility via Hazard-Informed Synthesized Envelopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14481)
*Alexei Odinokov,Rostislav Yavorskiy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种统一框架，通过跨层安全转换过程和危险感知的安全包络，实现城市环境中异构机器人系统的安全运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂的机器人-人类交互场景中，确保异构机器人系统在城市区域部署时的安全性是一个关键挑战。现有方法通常将安全性视为孤立的静态约束，缺乏跨层次的协同机制。

Method: 设计了一个统一框架，结合系统性危险分析与运行时强制执行，通过符号、空间和动态世界模型之间的跨层安全转换过程，生成并利用危险感知的安全包络。该框架与物理AI运行时系统集成，实现实时安全保障。

Result: 验证了该框架能够有效连接不同层次的世界模型，并与物理AI运行时系统自然对接，保障城市移动中的安全性。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架为异构机器人系统在复杂城市环境中的安全运行提供了系统化、可扩展的解决方案，推动了安全性从静态约束向动态协同的转变。

Abstract: As heterogeneous robotic systems deploy across diverse urban zones, maintaining safety amid complex human-robot interactions remains a critical challenge. We present a unified framework that bridges systematic hazard analysis and runtime enforcement using hazard-informed safety envelopes. Rather than treating safety as a static constraint isolated within individual software modules, we introduce a cross-layer safety transformation process spanning symbolic, spatial, and dynamic world models. We show how this representation naturally interfaces with physical AI runtime harnesses to guarantee safe urban mobility.

</details>


### [125] [Spatiotemporal Tube-Based Safety-Certificate for Autonomous Navigation of Articulated Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14531)
*Mohd. Faizuddin Faruqui,Ratnangshu Das,Ravi Kumar L,Pushpak Jagtap*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于时空管状通道的路径规划方法，用于铰接式车辆（如牵引车-半挂车、自动导引车等）的自主导航，确保拖车序列在允许修正范围内始终处于道路区域内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铰接式车辆在狭窄路线中操纵困难，因其物理特性和运动约束带来了自主导航挑战，需要一种能保证安全路径规划的方法。

Method: 采用时空管状通道方法，结合铰接单元的运动学模型和摆动约束，为牵引车生成安全通道，并通过允许的修正调整该通道以提供路径安全性认证。

Result: 所提方法在复杂路线的卡车-拖车仿真中得到验证，能够有效生成满足道路限制的安全路径。

Conclusion: 该方法可为多种铰接式车辆提供可靠的自主导航路径规划，具有实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Articulated vehicles are the workhorses of freight transportation, and their autonomous navigation is challenging. Their physical characteristics and motion constraints pose significant challenges in manoeuvring these vehicles on narrow routes. This paper presents a spatiotemporal tube-based approach to plan autonomous navigation of vehicles like tractor semi-trailers, truck/ tractor trailers, towing Automated Guided Vehicles (AGVs), and road trains. This planning approach provides a certified path plan for the truck or tractor, ensuring that the towed series of trailers always remains within the road corridor, limited by permissible corrections. The planning leverages the kinematics of the linked elements along with sway constraints to arrive at a safe tube for the actuated prime mover. We modify the spatiotemporal tube using permissible corrections to provide a route safety certificate to the vehicle for the given route. The proposed planning method is verified on a truck-trailer navigation simulation for a complex route.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [126] [Inducing Reward-Free Judging Rubrics that Reduce Over-Crediting in Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13564)
*Darragh Quinn,David Dylan,Roisin Healy,Fionn Carroll,Maeve Donnelly,Cormac Sheehan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RubricForge是一种通过从少量标注轨迹中生成评判标准来评估语言模型代理的方法，其目标是提高对环境奖励的忠实度，减少误判失败轨迹为成功的概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动评判方法（如G-Eval）容易将流畅但实际失败的代理行为误判为成功，缺乏对真实结果的忠实性，因此需要一种更可靠、可解释且无需奖励信号的评判机制。

Method: 提出RubricForge方法，通过在少量具有真实标签的轨迹上进行反思性演化，自动生成并优化评判标准文本；该标准被冻结后用于无环境访问情况下的代理评估，所有判断均可归因于明确的标准。

Result: 在tau-bench和WebShop数据集上，RubricForge相比G-Eval显著降低了误通过率（0.115 vs. 0.173），排名与真实结果更一致（Spearman 0.410 vs. 0.370），但在总体准确率上无显著差异，且绝对分数校准略逊于G-Eval。

Conclusion: RubricForge通过可读的、基于真实结果演化的评判标准，在不依赖环境奖励的情况下提升了评判的忠实性，尤其降低了部署中有害的误通过率，更适合实际应用。

Abstract: Evaluating language-model agents at scale increasingly relies on a second language model as an automatic judge, because the gold signal, an executable environment reward, is expensive, slow, or unavailable at deployment time. Such a judge is a reward-free proxy whose value depends on whether it can be trusted, yet existing judges either hand-write the scoring rubric, as in G-Eval, or fine-tune the judge's weights, and both tend to credit fluent but unsuccessful trajectories as successes. We instead induce the text of an agent-judging rubric from a small set of ground-truth-labeled trajectories, grounding it in true outcomes. We present RubricForge, which evolves a judge rubric by reflective evolution against labeled trajectories to maximize agreement with the environment reward, freezes it, and applies it to held-out trajectories in one model call with no environment access. The optimized artifact is human-readable text, so every verdict is attributable to named criteria. Using one frozen 7B model as both agent and judge, on tau-bench (173 labeled trajectories drawn from 220 rollouts) and WebShop (160), the principal gain is faithfulness rather than raw agreement. The edge over a generic G-Eval judge is not statistically significant (McNemar p = 0.248), and absolute-score calibration marginally favors the generic judge (|err| difference -0.048, p = 2x10^-4). Yet RubricForge over-credits failed trajectories roughly half as often (0.115 vs. 0.173 false-pass rate on tau-bench, with three over-credit catches and zero reversals) and ranks graded WebShop outcomes more faithfully (Spearman 0.410 vs. 0.370). For a reward-free evaluator the false-pass rate, not aggregate agreement, is the deployment-relevant quantity, since a false pass ships a broken agent whereas a false fail merely costs a retry.

</details>


### [127] [Depth-Aware Sensitivity Analysis of Mixture-of-Experts Models via Magnitude-Based Expert Masking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13565)
*Pradeep Kumar Sharma,Shantanu Godbole,Hritvik Shrivastava*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对Qwen3.6-35B-A3B模型的MoE层进行系统性敏感度分析，发现不同深度的层对专家掩码的敏感性存在显著差异：早期和中期层较为脆弱，而后期特别是末期层可容忍高强度掩码。基于此，晚期聚焦型掩码策略在保持输出质量的同时显著提升压缩效率，并为MoE模型的深度感知压缩与实际权重裁剪提供了实证基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管MoE架构被广泛用于扩展大语言模型并保持计算效率，但各MoE层的重要性尚未被充分刻画，尤其在模型压缩场景下缺乏对层敏感性的系统理解。

Method: 采用基于幅值的专家掩码方法，在XLCoST跨语言代码翻译基准上对Qwen3.6-35B-A3B模型（含40个MoE层，每层256专家，top-8路由）进行多阶段、多规模（100/300/500提示）的逐层敏感性分析，并评估不同掩码策略及top-k路由宽度缩减的影响。

Result: 发现层敏感性具有强深度依赖性：前层（0-9）和中层（10-29）对掩码敏感，后层（30-39）尤其是末层（35-39）可耐受高强度低幅值专家掩码；晚期聚焦策略在300提示下保留249-255/300优质输出并掩码640-1145专家，优于全层均匀掩码；在500提示验证集中，仅对35-39层施加50%掩码可保留419/500输出且仅掩码640/10240专家；将top-k从8减至6未损质量且显著降低延迟，但尚难与强掩码协同。

Conclusion: MoE模型的层敏感性呈深度依赖特性，支持采用针对晚期层的聚焦式专家掩码策略实现高效模型压缩；研究为基于激活评分、物理权重裁剪及训练恢复的深度感知MoE压缩提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures scale large language models (LLMs) while preserving computational efficiency through sparse activation. Despite their widespread adoption, the relative importance of individual MoE layers remains insufficiently characterized, particularly for model compression. This paper presents a systematic layer-wise sensitivity analysis of the Qwen3.6-35B-A3B model (40 MoE layers, 256 experts per layer, top-8 routing) using magnitude-based expert masking on the XLCoST cross-lingual code translation benchmark. We conduct a multi-phase study spanning 100, 300, and 500 prompt evaluation scales across three H100 GPU servers. Our central finding is that layer sensitivity is strongly depth-dependent: early layers (0-9) and middle layers (10-29) are highly fragile to expert masking, while late layers (30-39), and especially very-late layers (35-39), tolerate aggressive masking of low-magnitude experts. Flat all-layer masking at 30% retains only 150/300 Good+Similar outputs at 300-prompt scale, whereas late-focused policies retain 249-255/300 while masking 640-1,145 experts. On a later 500-prompt held-out validation slice, the narrow very-late policy (layers 35-39 @ 50%) achieves the strongest quality/masked-expert tradeoff among tested candidates, retaining 419/500 Good+Similar outputs while masking only 640 of 10,240 total experts. We additionally characterize top-k routing width reduction from 8 to 6 active experts per token, which shows a large observed wall-clock reduction on a 100-prompt probe with no Good+Similar loss, though it does not yet compose cleanly with aggressive expert masking. These findings provide an empirical foundation for depth-aware MoE expert masking and establish a practical path toward physical weight surgery, activation-based expert scoring, and training-based recovery.

</details>


### [128] [Modular Cognitive Architecture Emerges in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13567)
*Pengrui Han,Jacob Andreas,Evelina Fedorenko,Andrea Gregor de Varda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究表明，大语言模型（LLM）在处理不同认知任务时展现出与人类大脑相似的模块化结构，提示智能系统可能普遍具有功能特化的根本特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究智能系统的模块化组织是智能的本质特征还是生物大脑的进化偶然现象。

Method: 通过对46个涵盖四个认知领域（语言、形式推理、社会推理、物理推理）的任务进行电路分析，比较大语言模型中神经元的激活模式与人类大脑功能网络的对应关系。

Result: 发现大语言模型发展出与人类脑网络相对应的模块化架构：在人类中依赖相同脑网络的任务在LLM中也激活重叠的神经元，而依赖不同网络的任务则激活不同的神经元。

Conclusion: 大脑与人工神经网络中模块化的趋同演化表明，功能模块化可能是智能系统的一个基本属性。

Abstract: The human brain exhibits a striking degree of functional specialization, with distinct networks supporting language, formal reasoning, reasoning about other minds, and reasoning about the physical world. Is this modular organization a fundamental principle of how intelligent systems must be built, or an evolutionary accident specific to biological brains? Here, we test whether a similar organization emerges in Large Language Models--another class of intelligent systems created through a very different optimization process. Using circuit analyses across N=46 tasks spanning four cognitive domains (language, formal reasoning, social reasoning, physical reasoning), we find that LLMs develop a modular architecture that mirrors the human brain: tasks drawing on the same network in humans recruit overlapping neurons in LLMs, whereas tasks drawing on different networks recruit distinct neurons. The convergent emergence of modularity in brains and neural networks suggests that it may be a fundamental property of intelligent systems.

</details>


### [129] [A Year in LLM Serving: Workload Evolution, Caching and Load-Balancing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13573)
*William Nixon,Jon Durbin,Florian Standhartinger,Haryadi S. Gunawi,Juncheng Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过分析来自Chutes的一年期生产级大语言模型（LLM）服务轨迹，深入揭示了真实世界中LLM工作负载的演化特征和用户-模型交互结构，并计划公开完整数据集以推动后续研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务工作负载研究在时间和范围上受限，难以全面反映生产环境中用户与模型的动态交互行为，缺乏对长期演化的理解。

Method: 基于Chutes平台为期一年的完整生产轨迹，从整体、时间、模型层面和用户层面进行全局刻画与纵向分析，覆盖主流及长尾模型的真实使用情况。

Result: 揭示了LLM服务工作负载随时间演变的规律以及用户与不同模型之间的交互模式，发现了聚合视图下通常被掩盖的工作负载结构性特征。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了对现实世界LLM服务工作负载更深层次的理解，发布的完整轨迹数据将支持未来关于生产行为的研究，减少对采样或合成数据的依赖。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) serving has become a critical cloud workload, and realistic traces are essential for motivating and benchmarking serving systems. However, existing LLM serving workload studies remain limited in scale and scope. They often observe short time periods and provide limited visibility into how users interact with models in production. As a result, they do not fully capture how LLM serving workloads evolve over time or how user-model interactions shape production traffic.
  In this work, we further the understanding of real-world LLM serving workloads through both a global characterization and a longitudinal study of a one-year production trace from Chutes. Unlike prior studies, our trace captures full production behavior across many models and users, including both popular and long-tail models. We analyze the workload from aggregate, temporal, model-level, and user-level perspectives, revealing workload evolution and user-model structure that are typically hidden behind aggregate views. To support future research, we will release the full one-year trace with the paper, enabling downstream studies of production behavior without relying on sampled or synthetically generated workloads.

</details>


### [130] [Agentao: A Governed Local-First Runtime for Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13574)
*Bo Jin,Qiang Jiao,Xin Tong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Agentao，一个面向工具使用的LLM代理的受控本地优先运行时，通过分层架构实现权限分离与执行控制，提升代理的可管理性与可审查性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM代理在本地状态、持久化记忆和外部协议交互方面能力增强，也带来了权限过高、审计困难、提示注入等安全风险，亟需一种可治理的运行时环境。

Method: 设计了一个包含主机接口面、主机合约、运行核心、权限管控工具系统及多个支持子系统的分层架构，将模型生成的动作提议与主机授权执行分离。

Result: 实现了对权限、状态、协议边界和执行轨迹的显式运行时抽象，增强了代理的可治理性和可检查性，适用于主机控制的本地环境。

Conclusion: Agentao虽不提供形式化的安全保证，但展示了如何通过结构化设计提升LLM代理在复杂环境中的可控性与透明度。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly operate as execution systems that invoke tools, modify local state, use persistent memory, and interact with external protocols. These capabilities make agents useful, but they also introduce risks related to over-privileged actions, weak auditability, prompt injection, tool poisoning, and uncontrolled side effects. This paper presents Agentao, a governed local-first runtime for tool-using LLM agents. Agentao separates model-generated action proposals from host-authorized execution through a layered architecture consisting of host-facing surfaces, a host contract, a runtime core, a permission-mediated tool system, and supporting subsystems for memory, replay, plugins, skills, sub-agents, and protocol integration. We describe the motivation, threat model, design goals, governance model, execution pipeline, and structured event interface of the system. Agentao does not provide formal safety guarantees; rather, it demonstrates how permissions, state, protocol boundaries, and execution traces can be made explicit runtime abstractions for building agents that are more governable, inspectable, and suitable for host-controlled local environments. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/jin-bo/agentao.

</details>


### [131] [AI Evaluation Should Work With Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13577)
*Jan Kulveit,Gavin Leech,Tomáš Gavenčiak,Raymond Douglas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张AI评估应从追求超越人类的自主性能转向评估人机协作的表现，以促进AI系统更好地补充人类能力，从而带来更佳的社会效益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI评估范式强调取代人类，可能导致发展方向偏离真正有益于社会的目标。

Method: 提出一种新的评估范式，强调对人-AI团队整体表现的评价。

Result: 倡导将AI作为人类的补充而非替代，推动更积极的社会影响。

Conclusion: AI社区应转向以人机协作为核心的评估标准，以实现更优的长期社会价值。

Abstract: This position paper argues that the dominant paradigm of AI evaluation (which focuses on superhuman autonomous performance and so implicitly targets the goal of replacing humans) is guiding AI development in the wrong direction. Instead, the AI community should pivot to evaluating the performance of human--AI teams. We argue that this collaborative shift will foster AI systems that act as true complements to human capabilities and therefore lead to far better societal outcomes than will the current process.

</details>


### [132] [Stable Miscalibration in Large Language Models: A Practical View of High-Confidence Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13591)
*Akira Okutomi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了大语言模型中高置信度错误的稳定性问题，提出这些错误可能源于稳定的校准不良而非推理脆弱性。通过结合输出层审计和内部敏感性探测，发现自我批判性提示能减少隐藏状态的敏感性，表明提示可诱导局部稳定，但并不意味着整体校准良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上将大语言模型中的高置信错误归因于推理脆弱性，本文旨在检验另一种可能性：即这些错误是否源于模型内在的稳定但错误的校准状态。

Method: 采用标签感知的输出层审计分数与内部敏感性探针相结合的方法，评估多领域二元事实数据集上模型在强制回答基线下的置信度变化和隐藏状态变动。

Result: 审计分数能有效识别出自我批判提示降低决策损失的领域；自我批判提示显著降低了三个开源模型各层的隐藏状态敏感性；但高置信错误与正确答案在局部敏感性上无明显差异。

Conclusion: 高置信度错误可能是稳定且未校准的，而非单纯脆弱推理的结果，提示工程可通过局部稳定化影响模型行为，但这不等于实现了良好校准。

Abstract: High-confidence errors in large language models are often treated as evidence of fragile internal inference. We study a different possibility: stable miscalibration, where a confident wrong answer remains locally stable under small perturbations. We combine two diagnostics: a label-aware output-level audit score that ranks domains by confidence variation and overconfident mistakes under a forced-answer baseline, and an internal sensitivity probe that measures hidden-state movement. On a multi-domain binary factual audit set, this audit score tracks where abstention-aware self-critique reduces decision loss, although direct labeled baselines rank the same gain more strongly. Internally, self-critical prompting consistently reduces hidden-state sensitivity across layers in three open-weight models. This supports prompt-induced local stabilization rather than a purely output-level abstention pattern, but it does not imply calibration: audit-defined overconfident errors are not clearly more locally sensitive than confidently correct answers, so some high-confidence errors may be stable and miscalibrated rather than simply fragile.

</details>


### [133] [Measuring Cross-Task Behavioral Consistency in Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13598)
*Amritesh Banerjee,Pranil Raichura*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了行为一致性度量（BCM），用于量化智能体在不同任务间的行为一致性，发现跨任务与任务内的一致性是两个可分离的维度，且一致性不能简化为成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的智能体评估主要依赖于成功率等结果指标，缺乏对行为一致性的衡量，而行为一致性是反映智能体可靠性的关键过程指标。

Method: 提出行为一致性度量（BCM），通过训练模型从执行轨迹中提取行为特征，计算每条轨迹的特征归因向量，并测量同一系统内向量间的平均成对相似性。

Result: 在约9000条来自六个语言模型智能体的轨迹上验证，发现跨任务和任务内一致性是可分离的；某些系统在单一任务内行为一致但跨任务不一致，反之亦然；一致性与成功率无直接关联，且前沿模型与开源模型在任务内控制下仍存在一致性差距。

Conclusion: BCM提供了一种补充结果指标的过程级可靠性信号，揭示了智能体行为一致性的多维本质，建议未来评估应同时考虑结果和过程一致性。

Abstract: Agent evaluation relies almost entirely on outcome metrics such as success rate, which capture whether an agent succeeds but not how consistently it behaves. We argue that behavioral consistency across tasks is a distinct and measurable property, and we introduce the Behavioral Consistency Metric (BCM) to quantify it. BCM trains a model to predict task success from behavioral features of agent execution traces, derives a per-trajectory feature-attribution vector, and measures the mean pairwise similarity of these vectors within an agent system. Across roughly 9,000 trajectories from six language model agents on software engineering tasks, our central finding is that cross-task and within-task consistency are distinct axes that can diverge: some systems are locally reproducible, behaving similarly on repeated attempts at one task, yet globally fragmented, with no stable strategy across different tasks, while others are consistent at both scales. Prior work measures only same-task reproducibility and so cannot observe this separation. We further find that consistency is not reducible to success rate, since systems with comparable success can differ sharply in consistency, and that the frontier-versus-open-source consistency gap persists under a within-task control that holds task difficulty constant. We position BCM as a process-level reliability signal that complements outcome metrics, and we are explicit about the conditions under which it is meaningful.

</details>


### [134] [Cross-Disciplinary Taxonomy and Modeling of Misunderstanding Generation, Amplification, and Detection, from Pragmatics to AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13604)
*Babak Abbaschian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的误解检测分析框架，将误解视为一个分层过程，识别出11种精确的失败模式，并在八个分析层面上进行建模，扩展了信息与通信理论的应用范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于沟通越来越多地通过AI中介渠道进行，传统的实时面对面交流资源减少，导致误解难以及时发现和修复，因此需要系统性理解误解产生的机制。

Method: 整合九个不同研究领域的文献，归纳出误解发生的分层过程，识别出11种失败模式，并建立包含八个分析层的正式模型；同时提供可审计的证据矩阵、编码手册和九个对话案例分析。

Result: 提出了八个分析层，其中八个机制主要引发分歧，两个放大已有分歧，一个决定分歧能否被检测和修复；建立了首个将机制定位到具体沟通环节并按功能分类的误解分类体系。

Conclusion: 该分层模型为理解和检测AI-mediated沟通中的误解提供了理论基础和实用工具，推动了从信号传输到意义重建的通信理论发展。

Abstract: Detection of misunderstanding is an urgent problem to solve because communication has moved away from real-time, in-person interaction and is increasingly handled by AI-mediated channels. This shift cuts communicators off from the resources repair depends on faster than new means of detection are being built. In this paper we analyse misunderstanding as a layered process in which a divergence is generated, may then be amplified, and is either detected and repaired or left to persist unnoticed. Consolidating accounts from nine fields of research that do not ordinarily cite one another, we identify eleven exact failure modes and show that each operates at a specific point in a communicative process rather than anywhere within it. Those points give eight analytical layers, derived from the literature rather than adopted from an existing model. Eight of the mechanisms primarily generate a divergence, two primarily amplify one already present, and one governs whether a divergence is detected and repaired. We model the eight layers formally, extending information and communication theory from the transmission of signals to the reconstruction of meaning, and we supply a source-by-source evidence matrix that makes every rating auditable, a coding manual, and nine analysed dialogue cases. No prior classification of misunderstanding both locates mechanisms at points in the process and types them by function.

</details>


### [135] [Active Perception for Embodied Disambiguation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13605)
*Yiwei Liu,Luwei Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于主动感知的具身目标消歧框架，结合视觉-语言模型通过主动观察、用户澄清和目标选择来解决实体环境中的目标歧义问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具身环境中，自然语言指令存在目标歧义，这种歧义不仅来自用户意图，还源于当前观测中缺乏任务相关的物理证据（如遮挡、视角受限等），现有方法主要依赖询问用户，缺乏主动获取观测信息的能力。

Method: 提出一个主动感知框架，以主动观测为核心，利用视觉-语言模型根据累积的视觉证据和交互信息，动态决策继续观测、请求澄清或完成目标选择。

Result: 真实机器人实验表明，该框架能有效结合物理信息获取与用户意图澄清，形成统一的具身消歧过程，提升目标识别准确率。

Conclusion: 主动观测不仅能直接恢复缺失的判别性视觉证据，还能揭示物体名称、标签和语义属性，在必要时改善用户澄清效果，是解决复杂环境中语言指令歧义的有效途径。

Abstract: Natural language provides robots with a flexible task interface, but target ambiguity in embodied environments arises not only from user intent; it can also result from missing taskrelevant physical evidence in the current observation. Existing interactive disambiguation methods primarily obtain additional information by asking the user, whereas occlusion, restricted viewpoints, unreadable text, and unobserved targets require the robot to actively change its observation. We propose an active-perception framework for embodied target disambiguation that uses active observation as the backbone for information acquisition and uses a vision-language model to decide, on the basis of accumulated visual evidence and interaction information, whether to continue observing, request clarification, or complete target selection. Active observation can both directly recover missing discriminative evidence and reveal object names, labels, and semantic attributes, thereby improving user clarification when it remains necessary. Real-robot experiments show that the framework combines physical information acquisition and userintent clarification within a unified embodied disambiguation process.

</details>


### [136] [MobileMem: Learning from a Year of Mobile Experiences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13606)
*Xinle Deng,Yida Xue,Xiangyuan Ru,Haoming Xu,Shuofei Qiao,Mengru Wang,Yijun Chen,Buqiang Xu,Chen Jiang,Yuchen Eleanor Jiang,Lizhong Wang,Jianfeng Wang,Li Zeng,Haofen Wang,Guilin Qi,Huajun Chen,Ningyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MobileMem是一个用于研究设备上长期记忆的基准和框架，基于大规模移动体验数据，支持多模态、多跳和时序推理等任务，推动AI助手实现持续个性化学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的记忆基准无法满足真实移动场景中对异构、多模态、动态且高度个性化的用户经验建模的需求，因此需要一个更贴近现实的长期记忆评估框架。

Method: 提出MobileMem，通过知识引导的合成流程，从用户应用会话中构建连贯且时间一致的长周期行为轨迹，并提供文本与多模态两种设置，支持多跳推理、时序理解、知识更新和隐式偏好推断。

Result: MobileMem能够有效支持AI代理回忆过去、理解当前并适应未来，在模拟真实移动使用情境下实现了对长期个性化经验的记忆与利用。

Conclusion: MobileMem将记忆从信息检索转向基于经验的智能，为持续个人化学习提供了新的基准和研究方向。

Abstract: The next generation of AI agents is increasingly moving beyond systems that answer isolated questions toward persistent personal assistants that can understand, remember, and continuously learn from users' experiences. Such assistants require long-term memory to accumulate and leverage user-specific experiences over time, yet existing benchmarks remain inadequate for realistic mobile settings, where experiences are heterogeneous, multimodal, evolving, and deeply personal. We introduce MobileMem, a benchmark and framework for studying on-device long-term memory, grounded in a year-scale collection of mobile experiences. MobileMem employs a knowledge-grounded synthesis pipeline to construct coherent and temporally consistent long-horizon trajectories from user-app sessions. It provides complementary text and multimodal settings covering multi-hop and temporal reasoning, knowledge updating, and implicit preference inference. Specifically, MobileMem enables agents to remember the past, understand the present, and adapt to the future. By modeling experiences rather than isolated facts, MobileMem moves memory beyond information retrieval toward experiential intelligence for continuous personal learning.

</details>


### [137] [No Universal Signal Predicts Sample-Level LLM Regression under Version Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13607)
*Jia Sheng,Yiwei Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究了如何利用推理时的信号预测大语言模型更新后的样本级性能退化，发现不同任务中各类信号的有效性存在差异，且跨版本信号在某些情况下优于单一模型的置信度信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管前沿大语言模型整体性能不断提升，但个体样本可能出现退化，即原本正确的输出在新模型中变为错误。本文旨在探究如何在推理阶段预测这种退化现象。

Method: 比较了单模型信号（如置信度、logit margin、注意力熵）与跨版本信号（如输出KL散度、似然漂移、token级KL、表示漂移）在统一的附加值测试框架下的表现，评估各信号相对于置信度基线的增益。

Result: 在六个基准数据集、三类任务（多项选择题、数学推理、代码生成）和六组模型更新对上发现：(1) 信号有效性依赖于任务类型，置信度在MCQ和简单数学任务上表现最好，而似然/KL类信号在更难的数学和代码任务中更有效；(2) 没有一种信号在所有模型更新中都最优；(3) 即使在置信度失效的情况下，某些跨版本信号仍具信息量，可用于无标签场景下的选择性回退机制。

Conclusion: 实践者可根据具体任务类型和模型更新情况，选择最可信的退化预测信号，并结合选择性回退策略缓解模型更新带来的负面风险。

Abstract: Frontier LLMs are updated frequently and typically outperform their predecessors in aggregate. But aggregate gains say little about individual samples: an update can still cause sample-level regression, where a response correct under the old model becomes incorrect under the new one. This paper studies how to predict such regressions from signals available at inference time. We compare single-model signals (confidence, logit margin, attention entropy) against cross-version signals (output KL divergence, likelihood drift, token-level KL, representation drift) under a unified added-value test that isolates each signal's gain over a confidence baseline. Across six benchmarks in three task families (multiple-choice question answering, or MCQ; math reasoning; code generation) and six model update pairs, we find that (1) signal effectiveness is task-dependent: confidence is strongest on MCQ and simpler math, while likelihood/KL signals give the most frequent gains on harder math and code; (2) no signal is universally best across model updates either; and (3) some cross-version signals stay informative even when confidence fails, including without labels, which supports a proof-of-concept selective fallback that routes high-risk samples back to the old model. Practitioners can use these task-level patterns to choose which regression signal to trust for a given update. Code is available at https://github.com/jiashengsally/llm-regression-signals.

</details>


### [138] [Evaluating Agentic Learning Harness Capabilities Without Labels via the Scaling Hypothesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13608)
*Aryan Luthra,Kshitij Jain,Siddharth Arya,Bobby Filar,Anna Bertiger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种无需标注基准的端到端评估框架，利用更强的教师模型提供稀疏修正，通过学生模型在持续学习中向教师收敛的程度来评估学习框架的有效性，并验证了该方法在安全任务中的代理提升效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于标注基准的评估方法在操作安全场景中因标签稀缺、过时且不具代表性而失效，且LLM作为评判者在评估能力相近的代理时无法提供有效信号，因此需要一种更可靠的无监督评估方式。

Method: 基于扩展假设，使用更强的教师模型对学生模型（配备持续学习框架）进行稀疏修正，通过测量学生模型随时间向教师模型的收敛程度来评估学习框架的效果，并在多种安全任务、模型族和框架设计中验证该指标与真实性能提升的相关性。

Result: 实验表明，学生模型相对于教师模型的提升与相对于保留黄金标准的提升显著相关，证明教师相对提升可作为无标签情况下真实提升的可靠代理；相比之下，同等规模模型间的LLM-as-a-judge无法产生可用信号。

Conclusion: 该框架为持续学习系统提供了一种有效的无监督评估方法，表明即使仅有稀疏高精度的人类反馈，也可用于提升类似规模教师模型的性能，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Agentic "Continual Learning Harnesses", systems that pair an LLM with retrieval or memory to improve from feedback without retraining, have shown growing value in cybersecurity. But their value is conventionally measured by gains against labeled benchmarks, an approach that often fails in operational security settings. Benchmark labels are scarce, stale, and unrepresentative, so a practitioner often cannot tell whether a given harness helps at all or which of two is better for their task. Traditional LLM-as-a-judge offers little signal because it is no stronger than the agent it evaluates, and distillation is unreliable on scarce, sporadic, and biased labels.
  We propose a framework for evaluating learning harnesses end-to-end without a labeled benchmark, grounded in the scaling hypothesis. A stronger teacher model provides sparsely sampled corrections to a smaller student with a continual learning harness. We score a harness by how much its student converges toward the teacher over time. Across security tasks, model families, and harness designs, we show that improvement relative to the teacher correlates with improvement relative to a held-out gold standard, validating teacher-relative lift as a proxy for true harness uplift when labels are absent. We further show that LLM-as-a-judge between similarly powered models yields no usable signal. These results suggest that a teacher-sized model can be improved through the same harness when humans provide the same kind of sparse, high-precision corrections.

</details>


### [139] [SemPlan: Benchmarking Structured Semantic Planning for LLM-Based Queries over Enterprise Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13612)
*Bruno Santos Teixeira*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SemPlan Benchmark评估了四种架构在企业数据自然语言接口中的表现，结果显示结构化语义请求与确定性规划（A3）在回答正确率上最优，但不同架构存在权衡，无一者在所有指标上全面领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了评估自然语言接口在企业数据中将模糊请求转化为可控、可执行行为的设计空间，需要一个标准化、可重复的基准来比较不同架构的性能，特别是在正确性、安全性、成本和多轮一致性方面的权衡。

Method: 提出SemPlan Benchmark，包含1800个英葡双语合成测试用例（其中1200个为固定科学评估子集），在相同模型配置下比较四种架构：直接SQL生成（A1）、有限工具代理基线（A2）、结构化语义请求生成加确定性规划执行（A3）、以及带澄清/状态感知的语义计划变体（A4）。通过4800条主记录评估回答正确率、策略合规性、API成本等指标，并在150个案例子集上测试结果稳定性。

Result: 整体回答正确率较低：A1为22.25%，A2为22.58%，A3为25.67%，A4为24.25%；A3在预设成对正确性分析中显著优于其他三者。A1的策略正确率最高，无效或不安全查询率最低；A4的平均API成本最低且误拒绝率最低。在150个案例的稳定性子集上，答案正确可重复性为92.00%至98.67%。

Conclusion: 不同架构之间存在明显权衡关系，增加结构化约束改变了失败模式和效率，但并未单调提升整体正确性，也无法彻底解决歧义或多轮状态一致性问题；因此应采用权衡视角而非追求单一最优架构。

Abstract: Natural-language interfaces to enterprise data must translate underspecified requests into governed, executable behavior while controlling invalid queries, policy failures, cost, and nondeterminism. SemPlan Benchmark evaluates this architectural design space with a deterministic synthetic bilingual benchmark containing 1,800 cases in English and Brazilian Portuguese; 1,200 cases form the frozen scientific evaluation subset. Four architectures are compared under the same model configuration: direct SQL generation (A1), a bounded tool-agent baseline (A2), structured semantic-request generation followed by deterministic planning and execution (A3), and a clarification/stateful semantic-plan variant (A4). Across 4,800 primary records, answer correctness was low in absolute terms: 22.25% for A1, 22.58% for A2, 25.67% for A3, and 24.25% for A4. A3 had the highest observed correctness and significantly exceeded A1, A2, and A4 in the pre-specified paired correctness analysis, while A1 retained the highest policy-correct rate and the lowest unsafe-or-invalid rate. A4 had the lowest mean API cost and lowest false-refusal rate. On a preselected 150-case stability subset, answer-correct repeatability ranged from 92.00% to 98.67%. The results support a trade-off interpretation rather than a universal ranking: additional structural constraints changed failure modes and efficiency, but did not monotonically improve correctness or solve ambiguity and multi-turn state consistency.

</details>


### [140] [How Compliant is Sepsis Treatment? An Expert-Guided Neuro-symbolic Pipeline for Generating Clinical Compliance Insights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13617)
*Himanshu Tripathi,Kaushik Roy,Subash Neupane,Shahram Rahimi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种专家引导的神经符号方法，结合大语言模型进行语义归一化和模糊推理系统评估脓毒症治疗依从性，在MIMIC-IV数据上发现抗生素给药时机为关键薄弱环节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床护理是否遵循循证协议是一个典型的神经符号问题，但安全关键场景下纯神经或纯符号方法均表现不足，需结合两者优势提升准确性和可解释性。

Method: 采用专家指导的流程：大语言模型仅用于将药物和微生物学文本映射到标准临床词汇，随后由Sugeno型模糊推理系统基于8条脓毒症生存运动规则进行推理，输出[0,1]区间的分级评分而非二值判断。

Result: 在2,438例MIMIC-IV v3.1脓毒症病例中应用显示：抗生素给药时机依从性最差（平均得分0.24，仅13%在1小时内完成），Hour-1 bundle执行率均值为36.7%，乳酸升高患者中有51%出现检测中断，高依从组ICU住院时间显著短于低依从组（3.8 vs 5.1天）。

Conclusion: 该方法通过分离语义解析与逻辑推理，实现了对复杂临床指南的可解释、细粒度合规性评估，揭示了脓毒症早期管理中的关键缺陷，适用于质量监控与临床改进。

Abstract: Verifying whether clinical care follows evidence-based protocols is a natural neuro-symbolic problem, yet the safety-critical setting defeats either paradigm alone. We present an expert-guided pipeline that constrains a large language model strictly to semantic normalization, mapping messy drug and microbiology strings onto a fixed clinical vocabulary, while a Sugeno fuzzy inference system reasons over the normalized events. The fuzzy layer encodes eight Surviving Sepsis Campaign bundle rules and replaces binary judgments with graded scores in [0,1]. Applied to 2,438 MIMIC-IV v3.1 sepsis episodes, it surfaces antibiotic timing as the most critical breakdown (mean 0.24, 13% within one hour), Hour-1 underperformance (mean 36.7%), a 51% elevated-lactate drop-off, and descriptive differences in ICU stay across compliance groups (3.8 versus 5.1 days).

</details>


### [141] [Algorithm Design and Physician Liability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13618)
*Shujie Luan,Shubhranshu Singh,Tinglong Dai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了在美国引入的医疗责任规则如何影响人工智能算法设计和医生使用AI的决策，特别是在不同患者群体间准确率差异的情况下。研究发现，责任规则可能导致对弱势群体患者的AI使用减少，且这种影响是非单调的；随着责任增加，医生对弱势群体使用AI的程度先下降后上升。强制要求算法在各群体间具有相等准确性可能适得其反，损害所有患者群体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人工智能在临床决策中的广泛应用，算法在不同患者群体间表现不一致的问题引发关注。本文旨在探讨现行责任规则如何影响AI企业的算法设计和医生的AI使用行为，以缓解因算法差异带来的不公平问题。

Method: 本文构建了一个理论模型，包含一个开发算法的AI企业与一名作为最终决策者的医生。AI企业为两个患者群体设计算法，提升弱势群体的准确性成本更高；医生权衡AI带来的不确定性降低与其可能承担的责任风险，决定是否使用AI。分析责任程度变化对企业投资策略和医生使用行为的影响。

Result: 研究发现责任规则导致AI使用的差异化：医生可能整体减少AI使用，并在中等责任水平下对弱势群体更少依赖AI。该效应是非单调的——随着责任加重，医生对弱势群体使用AI的程度先降后升，原因是企业逐渐转向缩小差距或采用等精度设计。此外，强制要求算法在各群体间达到相同精度会扭曲企业的投资激励和医生的实际使用决策。

Conclusion: 单纯通过责任追究或强制要求算法准确性平等，并不能有效改善临床AI应用中的公平性，反而可能带来 unintended 后果。政策制定需综合考虑激励机制与实际行为响应，避免对企业和医生造成扭曲激励，从而真正提升医疗AI的公平与效率。

Abstract: A single clinical algorithm can deliver unequal accuracy across patient groups, and concern about such disparity has grown as artificial intelligence (AI) spreads through clinical decision-making. In response, a liability rule introduced in the United States holds healthcare providers responsible when their reliance on disparate algorithms contributes to erroneous clinical decisions. We examine how such liability considerations reshape (i) an AI firm's algorithm design decisions that drive group-specific accuracy and (ii) a physician's decisions to use AI in healthcare delivery. The AI firm designs an algorithm for two patient groups, and improving accuracy for the disadvantaged group is more costly. The physician (who remains the accountable decision-maker) then decides whether to consult AI, weighing the reduction in clinical uncertainty against expected liability exposure when AI errors disproportionately affect the disadvantaged group. We find the liability rule can induce disparate use of AI: the physician may reduce AI use overall and, over an intermediate range of liability, rely on AI less for disadvantaged patients. The effect is non-monotone. As liability increases, the physician's use of AI for disadvantaged patients first declines, then rises as the firm reallocates investment toward reducing disparity or switches to an equal-accuracy design. Mandating equal algorithmic accuracy across patient groups can then inadvertently harm both groups, because a uniform accuracy requirement distorts the firm's investment incentives and the physician's equilibrium AI-use decisions.

</details>


### [142] [Your Probabilistic JEPA Is Secretly a Hidden Markov Model: A State-Space Interpretation of Joint-Embedding Predictive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13621)
*Yongchao Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了全时间索引的预测信息瓶颈VJEPA（PIB-VJEPA）与隐马尔可夫模型（HMM）在计算结构上的深刻对应关系，并提出了Markov-Chain JEPA（MCJEPA）以建立精确的序列级HMM等价性，为时间JEPA提供了有原则的状态空间解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索PIB-VJEPA与HMM之间的内在联系，旨在为时间JEPA提供一个清晰的状态空间模型解释，增强其理论基础和可解释性。

Method: 通过分析PIB-VJEPA的架构，识别出其与HMM相对应的三个角色：状态推断、状态转移和观测生成；提出MCJEPA模型，使用学习到的转移矩阵替代潜在预测器，在有限时间齐次情况下保证多步预测的一致性；扩展至条件离散状态转移、连续状态马尔可夫核及连续时间动态。

Result: 建立了PIB-VJEPA与HMM之间四种逐步增强的对应层次，并给出了实现精确序列级等价的充分条件；MCJEPA在特定条件下确保Chapman-Kolmogorov方程的精确满足；实验验证了过渡组合、滤波解释以及合成过程中的预测马尔可夫化。

Conclusion: PIB-VJEPA与HMM具有相同的计算结构，预测信息瓶颈学习可被理解为寻找紧凑的预测状态，该研究为时间JEPA提供了坚实的状态空间建模基础。

Abstract: A hidden Markov model (HMM) combines three roles: inference of a hidden-state belief from observations, propagation through a Markov transition, and emission back to observation space. We show that full, time-indexed Predictive Information Bottleneck VJEPA (PIB-VJEPA) exposes the same computational structure: a stochastic context encoder plays the role of an amortized filtering distribution, a probabilistic predictor defines latent-state dynamics, and a decoder, inverse target encoder, or induced implicit conditional supplies the emission direction. We distinguish 4 progressively stronger levels of correspondence and give sufficient conditions for exact sequence-level HMM equivalence. To make the connection concrete, we introduce Markov-Chain JEPA (MCJEPA), which replaces the latent predictor by a learned transition matrix; in the finite time-homogeneous case, matrix powers guarantee exact multi-horizon Chapman--Kolmogorov consistency. Conditioned discrete-state transitions, continuous-state Markov kernels, and continuous-time dynamics extend this construction, while deterministic temporal JEPA appears as a degenerate Dirac-kernel special case. We further interpret predictive information-bottleneck learning as seeking a compact predictive state: compression promotes minimality, while residual predictability tests sufficiency. Controlled experiments support transition composition, the filtering interpretation, predictive Markovization in a known synthetic process, and the distinction between JEPA latent prediction and HMM-style sequence learning. Together, these results give temporal JEPA a principled state-space interpretation.

</details>


### [143] [ARC: Fair Relative Advantage Comparison in Open-Ended Real-World Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13622)
*Yongqi Tong,Tan Li Hui Faith,Choy Zhen Wen Marcus,Zhou Jin,Kewei Fu,Jiang-Ming Yang,Jianshe Li,Xin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为ARC的训练方法，通过策略条件化的 rollout 分组、混合奖励和熵正则化，解决开放世界交互中基于奖励的学习存在的“奖励公平性问题”，并在新提出的交互范式Inter上验证了其在工具使用和响应速度上的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在开放世界的实际交互中，存在多种合理的交互行为，传统基于群体的强化学习假设 rollout 之间具有可比性，但在多策略场景下这一假设不成立，导致奖励模型偏好影响行为优化方向，而非根据上下文选择最合适的行为。因此，需要一种更公平的比较机制来确保优化过程的合理性。

Method: 提出ARC（Advantage Regularization via Conditioning）训练方案，采用策略条件化的 rollout 分组以实现行为可比性，结合混合奖励机制与熵正则化，提升优势估计的公平性；同时构建Inter交互范式及其配套数据集Inter-86K，支持监督学习与强化学习训练。

Result: 实验表明，ARC显著提升了τ/τ²工具使用基准性能，Inter将首 token 时间从4.91秒缩短至1.27秒，优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 开放世界交互学习的关键瓶颈不仅在于如何奖励智能体，更在于是否对其行为进行了公平比较；ARC通过条件化分组恢复了公平性，为多策略交互提供了更优的优化路径。

Abstract: Open-ended real-world interaction admits multiple valid behaviors: an agent may answer directly, ask for clarification, provide progress updates, or confirm before acting. This flexibility breaks a core assumption behind group-based RL: rollouts compared within a group are no longer guaranteed to be behaviorally comparable. As a result, reward-model preferences over interaction style can distort relative advantages and steer optimization toward reward-preferred behaviors rather than context-appropriate ones. We formalize this as a \textit{reward fairness problem} and propose \textbf{ARC} (Advantage Regularization via Conditioning), a training recipe that restores fairer relative comparison through strategy-conditioned rollout grouping, together with hybrid rewards and entropy regularization. We study ARC in our proposed \inter, a novel paradigm for responsive, steerable, and execution-aware user-agent interaction that decouples user-visible communication from latent reasoning and tool use. \inter\ also provides the annotation and distillation pipeline for constructing \inter-86K, our strategy-annotated training corpus for supervised and RL training. Empirically, ARC substantially strengthens the core $τ/τ^2$ tool-use benchmarks, while \inter\ reduces time-to-first-token from 4.91s to 1.27s relative to a think-style baseline. Together, these results suggest that a central bottleneck in open-ended interactive learning is not only how agents are rewarded, but whether their behaviors are compared fairly in the first place. The ARC implementation and \inter-86K training data will be released.

</details>


### [144] [Reward Machines for Signal Temporal Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13625)
*Alper Kamil Bozkurt,Shangtong Zhang,Yuichi Motai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于时序交替自动机的新型方法，用于从信号时序逻辑（STL）规范中进行强化学习控制合成，通过自动机接受条件导出马尔可夫奖励，有效解决了传统基于鲁棒性奖励方法在长时域嵌套时序算子下状态空间膨胀的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着现实世界系统复杂性的增加，手动控制器设计变得不可行，而许多现代自主和AI系统缺乏准确完整的模型，使得基于优化的方法不适用，因此需要基于学习的控制方法。现有将STL鲁棒性评分作为强化学习奖励的方法因依赖执行历史而导致状态空间急剧膨胀，尤其在处理具有任意嵌套时态算子的长周期任务时难以扩展。

Method: 本文提出一种基于自动机的方法：首先从给定的STL规范构建一个带时间的交替自动机，然后将自动机的位置和时钟值加入系统状态空间，并根据自动机的接受条件推导出适用于强化学习框架的马尔可夫奖励信号。

Result: 实验结果表明，与现有的基于鲁棒性奖励的方法相比，该方法训练出的策略具有更高的鲁棒性评分和规范满足率。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于自动机的记忆机制和马尔可夫奖励生成方法能够有效提升强化学习在STL规范引导下的控制策略学习效果，特别适用于复杂、长周期的实时系统控制任务。

Abstract: Signal temporal logic (STL) provides a formal language for specifying real-time properties of real-valued observations, along with a quantitative robustness score for monitoring satisfaction. Control synthesis from STL specifications is of interest since manual controller design becomes infeasible as real-world systems grow in complexity. Moreover, many modern autonomous and AI-enabled systems lack accurate and complete system models, which makes optimization-based synthesis approaches unsuitable and motivates learning-based control. Prior work uses STL robustness scores as rewards in reinforcement learning (RL) to obtain control policies satisfying given specifications; however, robustness depends on execution history, leading to intractable state space expansion for general long-horizon specifications with arbitrarily nested temporal operators. This work introduces a novel automata-based approach that provides an efficient memory mechanism and associated Markovian rewards suitable for RL frameworks. Our approach constructs a timed alternating automaton from the given STL specifications, augments the state space with automaton locations and clock valuations, and derives rewards from the automaton acceptance condition. We empirically demonstrate that our approach learns policies that achieve higher robustness scores and satisfaction rates than those learned by existing approaches using robustness-based rewards.

</details>


### [145] [A Calibrated Test of Internal Action Maps: State Signals Without Global Affine Closure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13626)
*Dekun Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在预训练模型Qwen/Qwen3-4B中，研究发现隐藏状态信号虽可解码或因果使用，但不必然支持可重用的动作映射；通过几何结构和干预实验验证，状态可用性、因果作用、局部几何与可重用闭包在测试载体中是可分离的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究神经网络中隐藏状态是否真正支持可重用的结构化动作映射，而非仅表面可解码或因果有效。

Method: 构建证据格（evidence lattice），在已知仿射S_5载体上验证几何分支，测试包括一步转移、组合性、逆操作、解码和交换性；对冻结的Qwen模型各层进行动作映射拟合与干预分析，并比较不同实体划分与重构数据集下的表现。

Result: 最终层h28的仿射映射在保持源信息时平均误差为0.519，跨域拟合为0.398；仅有23/30最强细胞触发闭包门；早期层h4/h16更适于一步转移但解码弱；因果效应仅出现在h28/h36；重训练可降低误差但无法通过组合性测试；有限世界学习保留代数信号但无源保持闭包。

Conclusion: 在所测载体中，状态的可访问性、因果可用性、局部几何结构与可重用闭包是分离的；结果限于特定模型、层、任务与函数类。

Abstract: A hidden state signal can be decodable or causally usable without supporting a reusable action map. We test whether action maps fitted without a source reach its natural post-action activation and compose. We organize the tests as an evidence lattice and validate the geometric branch on a known affine S_5 carrier: all held-source folds pass one-step, composition, inverse, decoding, and commutativity gates. Structured curvature and held-domain conjugacy raise error monotonically, but only 23/30 strongest cells flip a closure gate, bounding rather than universalizing calibration. In post-trained Qwen/Qwen3-4B, frozen final-token h28 affine maps have mean held-entity error .519, versus .398 for within-test-domain cross-fit. Seven randomized entity splits and map geometry do not support a purely entity-specific account. Earlier h4/h16 layers fit one-step transitions better, but h4 conflict-state decoding is weak and lexical controls remain unresolved. Three matched intervention datasets regenerated from one frozen checkpoint show causal effects only at h28/h36. Outcome-aware refitting improves h28 one-step error to .474 (.469 with weighting), yet no refit passes composition. Learned finite worlds likewise preserve relative algebraic signals or shared charts without held-source affine closure. Within the tested carriers, state availability, causal use, local geometry, and reusable closure are separable. The result is limited to one pretrained model, sampled final-token layers, two finite worlds, and the tested affine or diagnostic function classes.

</details>


### [146] [Exploring ESC Winners with Nested Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13630)
*Anurag Sharma,Marcel Nöhre,Gerd Stumme*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ConceptFlow是一个兼容scikit-learn的Python库，用于从多值形式背景构建和渲染嵌套线图，应用于1975至2025年欧洲歌唱大赛冠军数据，揭示投票模式与音乐特征之间的依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更好地理解和可视化多值形式背景下的概念结构，并探索复杂数据集中属性间的隐含关系，本文提出了ConceptFlow工具。

Method: 通过将属性划分为概念尺度，执行概念缩放，计算因子格，识别对应子直积的填充节点，并生成交互式可视化图。

Result: 在Eurovision Song Contest数据上的应用显示了投票支持与音乐特性（如节奏和调性）之间的关联，嵌套线图揭示了跨尺度的蕴含关系。

Conclusion: ConceptFlow能够有效揭示多维数据中不同概念尺度之间的依赖结构，为形式概念分析提供了直观且可交互的可视化手段。

Abstract: We present ConceptFlow, a scikit-learn-compatible Python library for Formal Concept Analysis that constructs and renders nested line diagrams from many-valued formal contexts. Given a many-valued context and a partition of its attributes into conceptual scales, ConceptFlow performs conceptual scaling, computes the factor lattices, identifies filled nodes of the corresponding subdirect product, and produces an interactive visualization.
